# gam-mcp 0.6.0 PR 3 — Strict Inputs, Annotations, HTTP Boundary

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Nothing reaches GAM unchecked and nothing reaches the endpoint unchallenged. Every tool input is a strict, fully described, annotated object; every date range is resolved in the date time zone and checked by the rules of the report it feeds; every query is checked against its report type before a report is created; a name that means two things is refused rather than guessed; and the HTTP endpoint checks the Host, a bearer token and the body size before the SDK sees a request. The server is proved end to end over both transports, and PR 1's, PR 2's and the re-review's follow-ups owned here are closed.

**Architecture:** `dates.ts` owns date resolution: one `DateRangeInput`, one `resolveDateRange` that returns `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` envelopes instead of throwing, and one rule per report family (`rangeRuleFor`). `tool-input.ts` holds the input pieces every tool shares (`dateRangeInput`, `dateField`, `filterInput`) and `describeInputError`; `json-schema.ts` turns a strict zod input into the advertised JSON Schema, reading zod's own `unknownKeys` so the schema cannot claim a strictness the parser lacks. `ToolDefinition` requires a strict input and annotations, so a tool missing either does not typecheck; `buildReportTool` resolves the range by the report type its query names; `executeReport` and `executeReportRows` refuse a query whose names its report type does not offer (`checkReportQuery`). `toolsFor(mode)` gives stdio 33 tools and HTTP 29, `buildMcpServer(ctx, { mode })` builds either, and `connectStdio(ctx, io?)` serves stdio over any streams. `connectHttp` checks Host (port-agnostic, as the SDK's middleware does), then the bearer token (SHA-256 digests compared with `timingSafeEqual`), then the body size, and `readHttpSettings` reads those settings with the same digits-only reader as every other integer setting. A table of valid arguments per tool (`tests/tool-table.ts`) drives the guard tests, and `tests/transports-e2e.test.ts` runs the real server over both transports against the fake GAM.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript (strict, ESM/nodenext), Node >= 22, zod 3.25, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.30.0, google-auth-library 9, vitest 4.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-17-gam-mcp-0.6.0-design.md` (§2.7, §3; tests G1–G5, H1–H3, H5, H6). Index: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-17-gam-mcp-0.6.0-index.md`. Previous PR: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-17-gam-mcp-0.6.0-pr2-identity-cache.md`. This plan also carries the four PR 1 follow-ups and the five PR 2 follow-ups the index assigns to PR 3, the pending-run fix and index note from PR 2's re-review, the parts of §4.4 and of tests I4, I5, I8, I11 and I14 whose behaviour lands here, and the owner's requirement that 0.6.0 work in both transport modes. The Self-Review Notes map each to a task.

## Global Constraints

- Node >= 22 (`engines`), TypeScript strict, ESM; relative imports end in `.js`. No new runtime dependencies. The SDK stays v1 (spec D-5); 1.30.0 is installed.
- `npm run typecheck` covers `src/` only. **Tests are not typechecked** — every task runs them, and a test file that is wrong at run time is wrong.
- Conventional Commits; breaking changes carry a `BREAKING CHANGE:` footer. **No `Co-Authored-By` line and no AI attribution in any commit.**
- Tests use network code `1234` and invented names only (generic-MCP rule). No tool description or fixture may imply a publisher's configuration.
- `tests/helpers.ts` `makeCtx()` is `Asia/Tokyo` at 2026-09-17T04:00:00Z: **today is 2026-09-17 and yesterday 2026-09-16**. Every historical range in a test must end by 2026-09-16, and every `sell_through` window must start on 2026-09-17 or later. The existing tool tests already satisfy this except the two `sell_through` cases Task 6 moves.
- Tool-call arguments are JSON, so an escape such as backslash-u-0001 typed into an Edit or Write argument is decoded into a raw control byte. Write non-printable characters with `String.fromCharCode` (tests) or `chr()` (scripts); no file in this plan contains one. Three places do contain backslash sequences that must survive being written — the regular expressions in `src/parse.ts` and `src/zones.ts`, and `DATE_PATTERN` in `src/json-schema.ts` — and Task 4's test compares that pattern, so a lost backslash fails a test rather than shipping. Task 15 scans the branch for raw control bytes with Python (macOS `grep` has no `-P`).
- **Do not change `tests/http-transport.test.ts`.** It has timed out once at 10 s under full-suite load; if it fails during a full run, re-run it alone (`npx vitest run tests/http-transport.test.ts`) before investigating anything else. Task 12 keeps `_internal.readJsonBody`'s signature so that file keeps passing unchanged, and verifies it with `git diff --exit-code`.
- `tests/setup.ts` fails any test whose fake GAM receives a write other than creating or running a report, or a create without `timeZoneSource: PUBLISHER` and the fake network's currency (H7, D5). Nothing in this PR sends a write; `ReportDefinition.timeZone` (Task 11) is read back, never sent.
- `tests/structure.test.ts` stays green: only `src/report-exec.ts` and `src/usage.ts` may contain `.pollOperation(`; the quoted `POST` literal appears only in `src/gam-client-rest.ts` inside `defineReport`, `runReport` and the retry-policy check; no quoted `PUT`, `PATCH` or `DELETE` anywhere in `src/`; and no file in `src/` contains `downloadReport`, `fromCsvGzFile`, `file_path` or `readCsvGz` — do not write those words in new code or comments.
- Time-dependent tests use injected clocks and sleeps, never real waits. The exceptions are the real process in `tests/cli-startup.test.ts` and the real sockets in `tests/http-boundary.test.ts` and `tests/transports-e2e.test.ts`, which wait on I/O, not on timers.
- **Never log a credential value or the bearer token.** The token is compared by hashing both sides with SHA-256 and comparing the digests with `crypto.timingSafeEqual`, which handles different lengths without throwing; logs name only the kind of refusal.
- Result key order and provenance key order are contracts (spec §1.1). `report_time_zone` becomes `string | null` in Task 11; nothing else about the order changes, and Task 9 adds a test that holds it for every tool.
- A known shortcut is marked with the codebase's `ponytail:` comment, as `pacing_status` and `find_anomalies` are in Task 3.
- Out of scope for this PR (do not implement): the rest of §4 — `top_n` sorts and `sorts` in the definition, `derived` blocks and `null` arithmetic, the pacing rewrite, `find_unused_ad_units`' ACTIVE/leaf rule, heuristic labelling, `health_check`'s `isError`, the prompt wording (PR 4); the README, the smoke script and the release notes (PR 5).

### Configuration in this PR

| Variable | Parsed in | Startup behaviour after this PR |
|---|---|---|
| `GAM_HTTP_PORT` | `readHttpSettings()` in `src/startup.ts` (Task 13) | Unset or empty: stdio mode. Otherwise digits only, 1–65,535; anything else exits 2, quoting the value. A port another process holds exits 2 naming `GAM_HTTP_PORT` (`HttpListenError`, Tasks 12 and 13) |
| `GAM_HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN` | `readHttpSettings()` (Task 13) | **New, optional.** Must be printable ASCII with no spaces, or exit 2 — the message names the variable and never quotes the value. Set: every request but `/healthz` must carry `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (scheme case-insensitive), else `401` with `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer`. Unset: one `http.unauthenticated` warning when the server starts listening. Set in stdio mode: one `config.ignored` warning. Never logged |
| `GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS` | `readHttpSettings()` (Task 13) | **New, optional.** Comma-separated host names without ports; entries are trimmed and lower-cased and empty ones dropped; blank means unset. An entry with a port, scheme, path or space exits 2, naming the entry. Set: a request whose `Host` names another host gets `403` (the port is ignored), checked before the token; `/healthz` is exempt. Set in stdio mode: one `config.ignored` warning |
| `GAM_REPORT_TIMEOUT_MS`, `GAM_REPORT_CACHE_TTL_MS` | `readSettings()` | Unchanged defaults and bounds, now read digits-only: `1e3`, ` 5000`, `0x10` and `60000.0` exit 2 (Task 13) |
| `GAM_PAGE_ROWS` | `resolvePageRows()` in `src/report-exec.ts` | Unchanged default and clamp, now digits-only, and its error quotes the value (Task 13) |
| `GAM_TIMEZONE` | `readSettings()`, `chooseDateTimeZone()` | Unchanged parsing. It now also decides what "today" and "yesterday" are for every date rule (Task 3) |
| `GAM_NETWORK_CODE`, credential, `GAM_REPORT_CACHE_DIR`, `GAM_LOG_LEVEL`, `GAM_SAVED_QUERIES_FILE`, `GAM_INLINE_ROW_THRESHOLD` | unchanged (PR 1, PR 2) | unchanged |

### SDK facts this plan relies on

Checked against the installed `node_modules/@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` (version 1.30.0 in its `package.json`):

- `StreamableHTTPServerTransportOptions` is an alias of `WebStandardStreamableHTTPServerTransportOptions` (`dist/esm/server/streamableHttp.d.ts:20`). Its `allowedHosts`, `allowedOrigins` and `enableDnsRebindingProtection` are each marked `@deprecated Use external middleware ... instead` (`dist/esm/server/webStandardStreamableHttp.d.ts:79-96`).
- When enabled, the transport compares the **whole** `Host` header, port included, against the list (`dist/esm/server/webStandardStreamableHttp.js:139-151`), so an entry `gam-mcp.internal` would refuse `gam-mcp.internal:8080`.
- The replacement is `hostHeaderValidation(allowedHostnames)` (`dist/esm/server/middleware/hostHeaderValidation.js:20-63`), Express middleware (`res.status(403).json(...)`, lines 24 and 40) that takes the host name with `new URL('http://' + hostHeader).hostname` (line 37) and ignores the port. This server uses `node:http` and adds no dependency, so Task 12 makes the same port-agnostic check itself and never sets the deprecated options; spec §2.7 is reworded to say so.
- `StdioServerTransport(_stdin?: Readable, _stdout?: Writable, options?)` (`dist/esm/server/stdio.d.ts:14`, `dist/esm/server/stdio.js:9`) — Task 8 passes streams to it. Its framing helpers `ReadBuffer` and `serializeMessage` (`dist/esm/shared/stdio.js:6,37`) are what Task 14's in-process client uses.
- `StreamableHTTPClientTransport` takes `requestInit` (`dist/esm/client/streamableHttp.d.ts:77`) — Task 14 sends the bearer token through it.
- `ToolAnnotations` (`dist/esm/types.d.ts:8083`, schema `dist/esm/types.js:1173-1211`): `readOnlyHint` defaults to false, `destructiveHint` to true and **`openWorldHint` to true** when absent, which is why the tools that never leave this process declare `openWorldHint: false` (Task 8).

## The per-tool rule table

Every registered tool, what its input becomes, and which task does it. "Range" is the field that takes a `dateRangeInput` and the rule `buildReportTool` (or the handler) applies to it; `historical` means "must end by yesterday", `forecast` means "must start today or later, dates only". "Internal range" means the tool resolves a range itself and takes no date field.

| Tool | File | Range (rule) | Strict input + descriptions | Annotations |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `top_advertisers` | `src/tools/top_advertisers.ts` | `date_range` (historical), Task 6 | Task 6 | `READS_GAM` (from `buildReportTool`), Task 8 |
| `top_line_items` | `src/tools/top_line_items.ts` | `date_range` (historical), Task 6 | Task 6 | `READS_GAM` (from `buildReportTool`), Task 8 |

The three annotation sets (spec §3.4): `READS_GAM` is `{ readOnlyHint: true, openWorldHint: true }`, `READS_LOCAL` is `{ readOnlyHint: true, openWorldHint: false }`, `WRITES_LOCAL` is `{ readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: true, openWorldHint: false }`.

**Task order.** Task 1 is the index; Task 2 is the pending-run fix PR 2's re-review found, before anything else moves. Tasks 3–5 are the checks the tools depend on (dates, the advertised schema and input errors, the report-type check). Tasks 6–8 walk the tools: the builder tools' ranges and inputs, the remaining tools' strict inputs, then annotations and the per-mode tool sets. Task 9 closes PR 1's error-path follow-ups, Task 10 is ambiguous names, Task 11 PR 2's zone follow-ups. Tasks 12 and 13 are the HTTP boundary and its settings, Task 14 the owner's both-transports test, Task 15 local verification.

```markdown
- **Between PR 3 and PR 4, `pacing_status` and `find_anomalies` pacing return `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` on the first day of a month**, because both still resolve `month_to_date`, which PR 3 refuses on the first day of its period. PR 4 moves both to `last_7_days` (spec §4.3). No release happens in between.
```

### Task 2: A pending run that leaves memory while its caller polls is recorded again

From PR 2's re-review. Since PR 2's final fix wave, `runOnce` records a run as `pending` once, right after `runReport` (`src/report-exec.ts`, the `putPending` call in `runOnce`). If the memory LRU drops that entry while the caller polls (1,000 other definitions were recorded meanwhile), a timeout, a quota error or a poll that could not reach GAM no longer writes it back, and the next identical call creates a second GAM job. The re-reviewer reproduced it: one job on the code before the fix wave, two after.

The fix: on any ending but `REPORT_FAILED`, write the pending entry back **only if the key has no live entry**, keeping the run's original start time (the resumed entry's `started_at`, or the stamp `putPending` now returns). "Only if absent" never overwrites a `done` entry (or a newer run) that another call recorded. The memory tier's capacity becomes an option so a test can force the eviction.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/cache.ts`, `src/report-exec.ts`, `tests/cache.test.ts`, `tests/report-shared-runs.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createReportCache`, `executeReport`, `counted`, `definitionHash`, `definitionFor`.
- Produces:
  - `src/cache.ts`: `createReportCache(opts)` accepts `maxEntries?: number` (default `CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES`); `ReportCache.putPending(key, entry): string | undefined` returns the `started_at` it recorded (undefined when the entry was refused); new `ReportCache.restorePending(key: string, entry: { report: string; operation: string; started_at?: string }): void`.
  - `src/report-exec.ts` (module-private): `runOnce` calls `restorePending` on every poll failure except `REPORT_FAILED`.

```ts
  if (!('error' in done)) return { report, result: done.result, operation, cached: null, resumed: pending !== undefined };
```

with:

```ts
  if (!('error' in done)) return { report, result: done.result, operation, cached: null, resumed: pending !== undefined };
  if (done.error.code === 'REPORT_FAILED') {
    ctx.reportCache?.delete(key, { operation });
  } else {
    // GAM may still be running it. The entry recorded when it started can
    // have been dropped from memory while this call polled.
    ctx.reportCache?.restorePending(key, { report, operation, ...(startedAt !== undefined && { started_at: startedAt }) });
  }
  return done;
```

```bash
A run was recorded as pending only when it started. If the memory tier
dropped that entry while the caller polled, a timeout left nothing
behind and the next identical call created a second report job. Every
poll failure but REPORT_FAILED now puts the entry back, with its first
start time, unless another call has recorded something for the key."
```

### Task 3: Every date range is checked in the date time zone (spec §3.2)

`src/dates.ts` is rewritten. `resolveDateRange(range, { timezone, now, rule, field })` returns a resolved range or an `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` envelope naming the field (`details.field`, and a reason that starts with the field name); it never throws. Absolute dates must be real `YYYY-MM-DD` dates. The `historical` rule requires `end ≤ yesterday`; the `forecast` rule (only `FUTURE_SELL_THROUGH`, via `rangeRuleFor`) requires `start ≥ today` and refuses relative ranges. Relative `today` is gone. `month_to_date`, `quarter_to_date` and `year_to_date` on the first day of their period are `no complete day in this period yet`. `previousYear` clamps to the month's length and keeps a month-end end; `previousPeriod` and `lastDays` move here from `src/report-run.ts` (which keeps only `numOr0` and `pct`, for PR 4 to delete).

`src/tool-input.ts` is new: `dateRangeInput(description)` is the one `{ start, end } | { relative }` input every range-taking tool uses; its dates are plain strings so that a bad date reaches `resolveDateRange` and comes back as `INVALID_DATE_RANGE`, not as a schema error. The tools that already took relative ranges (`run_report`, `kv_report`, `compare_periods`, `run_saved_query`) switch to it here; the report-builder tools follow in Task 6.

Every existing caller moves to the envelope-returning form. `pacing_status` and `find_anomalies` pacing still resolve `month_to_date`, so between this PR and PR 4 they return `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` on the first day of a month (recorded in the index by Task 1; PR 4 moves both to `last_7_days`, spec §4.3). The comment on each says so with the codebase's `ponytail:` marker.

Test clock: `makeCtx` is 2026-09-17T04:00:00Z in `Asia/Tokyo`, so today is 2026-09-17 and yesterday 2026-09-16. Every existing tool test already ends its historical ranges by then.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/tool-input.ts`, `tests/date-inputs.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/dates.ts`, `src/errors.ts`, `src/report-run.ts`, `src/tools/compare_periods.ts`, `src/tools/diagnose_fill_rate.ts`, `src/tools/diagnose_floor_pricing.ts`, `src/tools/find_anomalies.ts`, `src/tools/find_unused_ad_units.ts`, `src/tools/kv_report.ts`, `src/tools/pacing_status.ts`, `src/tools/run_report.ts`, `src/tools/saved_queries.ts`, `tests/dates.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `gamError`, `executeReport`, `executeReportRows`.
- Produces:
  - `src/dates.ts`: `RELATIVE_RANGES` (the nine names, no `today`); `type RelativeRange`; `type DateRangeInput = { start: string; end: string } | { relative: RelativeRange }`; `type ResolvedRange = { start: string; end: string }`; `type RangeRule = 'historical' | 'forecast'`; `rangeRuleFor(reportType: string | undefined): RangeRule`; `type ResolveOptions = { timezone: string; now?: Date; rule: RangeRule; field?: string }`; `resolveDateRange(range: DateRangeInput, opts: ResolveOptions): ResolvedRange | GamErrorEnvelope`; `isCalendarDate(s: string): boolean`; `lastDays(days: number, timezone: string, now?: Date): ResolvedRange`; `previousPeriod(r: ResolvedRange): ResolvedRange`; `previousYear(r: ResolvedRange): ResolvedRange`. The old `DateRange` type and the throwing signature are gone.
  - `src/tool-input.ts`: `dateField(description: string): z.ZodString`; `dateRangeInput(description: string)` (a zod union of two strict objects).
  - `src/report-run.ts`: `lookbackRange` and `previousPeriod` removed.
  - `src/errors.ts`: the `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` hint names both rules and `GAM_TIMEZONE`.

Replace the whole of `tests/dates.test.ts` with:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  RELATIVE_RANGES,
  isCalendarDate,
  lastDays,
  previousPeriod,
  previousYear,
  rangeRuleFor,
  resolveDateRange,
  type DateRangeInput,
  type RangeRule,
} from '../src/dates.js';

const TZ = 'Asia/Tokyo';
// 2026-09-17 12:00 in Hong Kong: today is the 17th, yesterday the 16th.
const NOW = new Date('2026-09-17T04:00:00Z');

function resolve(range: DateRangeInput, at: string | Date = NOW, rule: RangeRule = 'historical', timezone = TZ) {
  const now = typeof at === 'string' ? new Date(`${at}T04:00:00Z`) : at;
  return resolveDateRange(range, { timezone, now, rule });
}

function reasonOf(r: unknown): string {
  if (!r || typeof r !== 'object' || !('error' in r)) throw new Error(`expected an error, got ${JSON.stringify(r)}`);
  const { error } = r as { error: { code: string; details: { reason: string } } };
  expect(error.code).toBe('INVALID_DATE_RANGE');
  return error.details.reason;
}

describe('relative ranges (spec §3.2)', () => {
  it('offers no relative today', () => {
    expect(RELATIVE_RANGES).not.toContain('today');
    expect([...RELATIVE_RANGES].sort()).toEqual([
      'last_30_days', 'last_7_days', 'last_full_month', 'last_quarter', 'last_week',
      'month_to_date', 'quarter_to_date', 'year_to_date', 'yesterday',
    ]);
  });

  it.each([
    ['yesterday', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' }],
    ['last_7_days', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-09-10', end: '2026-09-16' }],
    ['last_30_days', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-08-18', end: '2026-09-16' }],
    ['last_week', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-09-07', end: '2026-09-13' }], // a Thursday
    ['last_week', '2026-09-13', { start: '2026-08-31', end: '2026-09-06' }], // a Sunday
    ['last_week', '2026-09-14', { start: '2026-09-07', end: '2026-09-13' }], // a Monday
    ['last_full_month', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-08-01', end: '2026-08-31' }],
    ['last_full_month', '2026-01-05', { start: '2025-12-01', end: '2025-12-31' }],
    ['month_to_date', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-16' }],
    ['month_to_date', '2026-09-02', { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-01' }],
    ['quarter_to_date', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-07-01', end: '2026-09-16' }],
    ['last_quarter', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-04-01', end: '2026-06-30' }],
    ['last_quarter', '2026-02-10', { start: '2025-10-01', end: '2025-12-31' }],
    ['year_to_date', '2026-09-17', { start: '2026-01-01', end: '2026-09-16' }],
  ] as const)('%s on %s', (relative, today, expected) => {
    expect(resolve({ relative }, today)).toEqual(expected);
  });

  it.each([
    ['month_to_date', '2026-09-01'],
    ['quarter_to_date', '2026-07-01'],
    ['year_to_date', '2026-01-01'],
  ] as const)('%s on %s has no complete day yet', (relative, today) => {
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ relative }, today))).toContain('no complete day in this period yet');
  });

  it('resolves in the date time zone (D6)', () => {
    const now = new Date('2026-09-16T16:30:00Z');
    expect(resolve({ relative: 'yesterday' }, now, 'historical', 'Asia/Seoul')).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' });
    expect(resolve({ relative: 'yesterday' }, now, 'historical', 'UTC')).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-15', end: '2026-09-15' });
  });
});

describe('absolute ranges (spec §3.2)', () => {
  it('passes a checked range through', () => {
    expect(resolve({ start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-16' })).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-16' });
  });

  it.each(['banana', '2026-02-30', '2026-2-3', '2026-13-01', '', '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z'])(
    'rejects %j as a date, naming the field',
    (bad) => {
      const r = resolveDateRange({ start: bad, end: '2026-09-02' }, { timezone: TZ, now: NOW, rule: 'historical', field: 'base_range' });
      expect(reasonOf(r)).toBe(`base_range: start ${JSON.stringify(bad)} is not a real date in YYYY-MM-DD form`);
      expect((r as { error: { details: { field: string } } }).error.details.field).toBe('base_range');
    },
  );

  it('rejects start after end', () => {
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ start: '2026-09-10', end: '2026-09-01' }))).toBe('date_range: start 2026-09-10 is after end 2026-09-01');
  });

  it('a historical range must end by yesterday in the date time zone', () => {
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-17' })))
      .toBe('date_range: end 2026-09-17 is after yesterday (2026-09-16) in Asia/Tokyo');
    // 16:30 UTC on the 16th is already the 17th in Asia/Seoul, not in UTC.
    const now = new Date('2026-09-16T16:30:00Z');
    expect(resolve({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' }, now, 'historical', 'Asia/Seoul')).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' });
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' }, now, 'historical', 'UTC'))).toContain('after yesterday (2026-09-15) in UTC');
  });

  it('a forecast must start today or later, and must be given as dates', () => {
    expect(resolve({ start: '2026-09-17', end: '2026-12-31' }, NOW, 'forecast')).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-17', end: '2026-12-31' });
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-30' }, NOW, 'forecast')))
      .toBe('date_range: start 2026-09-16 is before today (2026-09-17) in Asia/Tokyo; a forecast covers today and later');
    expect(reasonOf(resolve({ relative: 'last_7_days' }, NOW, 'forecast'))).toContain('not a relative range');
  });

  it('never throws', () => {
    expect(() => resolve({ start: 'x', end: 'y' })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => resolve({ relative: 'nope' } as never)).not.toThrow();
  });
});

describe('rangeRuleFor', () => {
  it('treats only FUTURE_SELL_THROUGH as a forecast', () => {
    expect(rangeRuleFor('FUTURE_SELL_THROUGH')).toBe('forecast');
    for (const t of ['HISTORICAL', 'REACH', 'AD_SPEED', 'PRIVACY_AND_MESSAGING', undefined]) {
      expect(rangeRuleFor(t)).toBe('historical');
    }
  });
});

describe('derived ranges', () => {
  it('I4: previous_year clamps to the month and keeps a month-end end', () => {
    expect(previousYear({ start: '2028-02-01', end: '2028-02-29' })).toEqual({ start: '2027-02-01', end: '2027-02-28' });
    expect(previousYear({ start: '2029-02-01', end: '2029-02-28' })).toEqual({ start: '2028-02-01', end: '2028-02-29' });
    expect(previousYear({ start: '2028-02-29', end: '2028-03-15' })).toEqual({ start: '2027-02-28', end: '2027-03-15' });
    expect(previousYear({ start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-16' })).toEqual({ start: '2025-09-01', end: '2025-09-16' });
  });

  it('previous_period keeps the equal-length window', () => {
    expect(previousPeriod({ start: '2026-09-10', end: '2026-09-16' })).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-03', end: '2026-09-09' });
    expect(previousPeriod({ start: '2026-03-01', end: '2026-03-01' })).toEqual({ start: '2026-02-28', end: '2026-02-28' });
  });

  it('lastDays ends yesterday in the date time zone', () => {
    expect(lastDays(1, TZ, NOW)).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-16' });
    expect(lastDays(14, TZ, NOW)).toEqual({ start: '2026-09-03', end: '2026-09-16' });
  });

  it('isCalendarDate', () => {
    expect(isCalendarDate('2028-02-29')).toBe(true);
    expect(isCalendarDate('2027-02-29')).toBe(false);
    expect(isCalendarDate('2026-04-31')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { runReportTool } from '../src/tools/run_report.js';
import { kvReportTool } from '../src/tools/kv_report.js';
import { comparePeriodsTool } from '../src/tools/compare_periods.js';
import { runSavedQueryTool } from '../src/tools/saved_queries.js';
import type { ToolDefinition } from '../src/report-tool-builder.js';
import { makeCtx, ok } from './helpers.js';

// makeCtx: Asia/Tokyo, 2026-09-17 12:00 local. Yesterday is 2026-09-16.
const TODAY = '2026-09-17';

// Tools that take a range, the field it arrives in, and valid arguments
// without it. Task 2 adds the report-builder tools.
const CASES: { tool: ToolDefinition<any>; field: string; rest: Record<string, unknown> }[] = [
  { tool: runReportTool, field: 'date_range', rest: { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] } },
  { tool: kvReportTool, field: 'date_range', rest: { kv: 'section=news', metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] } },
  {
    tool: comparePeriodsTool,
    field: 'base_range',
    rest: { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'], comparison: 'previous_period' },
  },
];

async function call(tool: ToolDefinition<any>, args: Record<string, unknown>, over = {}) {
  const parsed = tool.input.safeParse(args);
  if (!parsed.success) return { parseError: parsed.error };
  return tool.handler(parsed.data, makeCtx({}, over));
}

function dateError(r: unknown): { field: string; reason: string } {
  const e = (r as { error?: { code: string; details: { field: string; reason: string } } }).error;
  expect(e?.code, JSON.stringify(r)).toBe('INVALID_DATE_RANGE');
  return e!.details;
}

describe('every range-taking tool checks its dates (spec §3.2, I5)', () => {
  describe.each(CASES)('$tool.name', ({ tool, field, rest }) => {
    it.each([
      ['banana', { start: 'banana', end: '2026-09-02' }],
      ['an impossible date', { start: '2026-02-30', end: '2026-03-02' }],
      ['an end of today', { start: '2026-09-01', end: TODAY }],
      ['start after end', { start: '2026-09-05', end: '2026-09-01' }],
    ])('rejects %s', async (_label, range) => {
      expect(dateError(await call(tool, { ...rest, [field]: range })).field).toBe(field);
    });

    it('rejects month_to_date on the first of the month', async () => {
      const r = await call(tool, { ...rest, [field]: { relative: 'month_to_date' } }, { now: new Date('2026-09-01T04:00:00Z') });
      expect(dateError(r).reason).toContain('no complete day in this period yet');
    });

    it('does not accept relative today', async () => {
      expect(await call(tool, { ...rest, [field]: { relative: 'today' } })).toHaveProperty('parseError');
    });
  });
});

describe('run_report picks the date rule from the report type', () => {
  const sellThrough = { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['SELL_THROUGH_FORECASTED_IMPRESSIONS'] };

  it('a sell-through query must start today or later', async () => {
    const r = await call(runReportTool, { ...sellThrough, date_range: { start: '2026-09-16', end: '2026-09-30' } });
    expect(dateError(r).reason).toContain('before today (2026-09-17)');
  });

  it('a sell-through query cannot use a relative range', async () => {
    const r = await call(runReportTool, { ...sellThrough, date_range: { relative: 'yesterday' } });
    expect(dateError(r).reason).toContain('not a relative range');
  });

  it('run_saved_query applies the same rules', async () => {
    const savedQueries = {
      get: () => ({ name: 'q', saved_at: 'x', body: { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] } }),
    };
    const parsed = runSavedQueryTool.input.parse({ name: 'q', date_range: { start: '2026-09-01', end: TODAY } });
    const r = await runSavedQueryTool.handler(parsed, makeCtx({}, { savedQueries: savedQueries as never }));
    expect(dateError(r).field).toBe('date_range');
  });
});

describe('compare_periods comparison ranges', () => {
  const query = { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] };

  it('checks an explicit comparison range as a historical range', async () => {
    const r = await call(comparePeriodsTool, {
      ...query,
      base_range: { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-07' },
      comparison: { start: '2026-09-10', end: TODAY },
    });
    expect(dateError(r).field).toBe('comparison');
  });

  it('I4: previous_year for February 2028 compares with all of February 2027', async () => {
    const ctx = makeCtx({
      reports: [
        { query, dateRange: { start: '2028-02-01', end: '2028-02-29' }, rows: [{ AD_UNIT_ID: 1, IMPRESSIONS: 20 }] },
        { query, dateRange: { start: '2027-02-01', end: '2027-02-28' }, rows: [{ AD_UNIT_ID: 1, IMPRESSIONS: 10 }] },
      ],
    }, { now: new Date('2028-03-10T04:00:00Z') });
    const args = comparePeriodsTool.input.parse({
      ...query,
      base_range: { start: '2028-02-01', end: '2028-02-29' },
      comparison: 'previous_year',
    });
    const r = ok(await comparePeriodsTool.handler(args, ctx));
    expect(r.summary).toContain('prev=2027-02-01..2027-02-28');
  });
});
```

Replace the whole of `src/dates.ts` with:

```ts
import { gamError, type GamErrorEnvelope } from './errors.js';

// The relative ranges a caller may ask for (spec §3.2). Every one ends by
// yesterday: there is no `today`, because a day that has not ended has
// incomplete numbers.
export const RELATIVE_RANGES = [
  'yesterday',
  'last_7_days',
  'last_30_days',
  'last_week',
  'last_full_month',
  'month_to_date',
  'quarter_to_date',
  'last_quarter',
  'year_to_date',
] as const;

export type RelativeRange = (typeof RELATIVE_RANGES)[number];

// What a tool accepts: dates, or a range relative to today.
export type DateRangeInput = { start: string; end: string } | { relative: RelativeRange };

export type ResolvedRange = { start: string; end: string };

// The checks a range gets beyond start ≤ end (spec §3.2). A historical
// report must end by yesterday. A forecast must start today or later and be
// given as dates, because a past window returns nothing and an empty
// forecast must not read as zero.
export type RangeRule = 'historical' | 'forecast';

export function rangeRuleFor(reportType: string | undefined): RangeRule {
  return reportType === 'FUTURE_SELL_THROUGH' ? 'forecast' : 'historical';
}

export type ResolveOptions = {
  // The date time zone (GAM_TIMEZONE): what "today" is.
  timezone: string;
  now?: Date;
  rule: RangeRule;
  // The input field named in errors.
  field?: string;
};

// Resolves and checks a range in the date time zone. Every failure is an
// INVALID_DATE_RANGE envelope naming the field; nothing is thrown.
export function resolveDateRange(
  range: DateRangeInput,
  opts: ResolveOptions,
): ResolvedRange | GamErrorEnvelope {
  const field = opts.field ?? 'date_range';
  const invalid = (reason: string) => gamError('INVALID_DATE_RANGE', { field, reason: `${field}: ${reason}` });
  const today = todayIn(opts.timezone, opts.now);

  if ('relative' in range) {
    if (opts.rule === 'forecast') {
      return invalid('a forecast takes start and end dates from today on, not a relative range');
    }
    const resolved = relativeRange(range.relative, today);
    return typeof resolved === 'string' ? invalid(resolved) : resolved;
  }

  for (const key of ['start', 'end'] as const) {
    if (!isCalendarDate(range[key])) {
      return invalid(`${key} ${JSON.stringify(range[key])} is not a real date in YYYY-MM-DD form`);
    }
  }
  if (range.start > range.end) return invalid(`start ${range.start} is after end ${range.end}`);
  if (opts.rule === 'historical') {
    const yesterday = addDays(today, -1);
    if (range.end > yesterday) {
      return invalid(`end ${range.end} is after yesterday (${yesterday}) in ${opts.timezone}`);
    }
  } else if (range.start < today) {
    return invalid(`start ${range.start} is before today (${today}) in ${opts.timezone}; a forecast covers today and later`);
  }
  return { start: range.start, end: range.end };
}

// A YYYY-MM-DD string naming a day that exists (2026-02-30 does not).
export function isCalendarDate(s: string): boolean {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) return false;
  const [y, m, d] = s.split('-').map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
  return date.getUTCFullYear() === y && date.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && date.getUTCDate() === d;
}

// The last `days` complete days, ending yesterday in the date time zone.
export function lastDays(days: number, timezone: string, now?: Date): ResolvedRange {
  const yesterday = addDays(todayIn(timezone, now), -1);
  return { start: addDays(yesterday, -(days - 1)), end: yesterday };
}

// The equal-length window immediately before `r`.
export function previousPeriod(r: ResolvedRange): ResolvedRange {
  const end = addDays(r.start, -1);
  return { start: addDays(end, -daysBetween(r.start, r.end)), end };
}

// `r` one calendar year earlier. A day the earlier month lacks is clamped
// to its last day, and a range that ends on its month's last day still does
// (2028-02-01..2028-02-29 → 2027-02-01..2027-02-28).
export function previousYear(r: ResolvedRange): ResolvedRange {
  return { start: shiftYear(r.start, false), end: shiftYear(r.end, isLastDayOfMonth(r.end)) };
}

function relativeRange(name: RelativeRange, today: string): ResolvedRange | string {
  const yesterday = addDays(today, -1);
  const [y, m] = parts(today);
  // A period-to-date range has no complete day on its first day.
  const toDate = (start: string): ResolvedRange | string =>
    start <= yesterday ? { start, end: yesterday } : `no complete day in this period yet (${name} on ${today})`;
  const quarterStart = m - ((m - 1) % 3);
  switch (name) {
    case 'yesterday':
      return { start: yesterday, end: yesterday };
    case 'last_7_days':
      return lastOf(7, yesterday);
    case 'last_30_days':
      return lastOf(30, yesterday);
    case 'last_week': {
      // Monday to Sunday of the last complete week.
      const weekday = new Date(`${today}T00:00:00Z`).getUTCDay(); // 0 = Sunday
      const lastSunday = addDays(today, -(weekday === 0 ? 7 : weekday));
      return { start: addDays(lastSunday, -6), end: lastSunday };
    }
    case 'last_full_month': {
      const end = addDays(ymd(y, m, 1), -1);
      const [py, pm] = parts(end);
      return { start: ymd(py, pm, 1), end };
    }
    case 'month_to_date':
      return toDate(ymd(y, m, 1));
    case 'quarter_to_date':
      return toDate(ymd(y, quarterStart, 1));
    case 'last_quarter': {
      const end = addDays(ymd(y, quarterStart, 1), -1);
      const [py, pm] = parts(end);
      return { start: ymd(py, pm - 2, 1), end };
    }
    case 'year_to_date':
      return toDate(ymd(y, 1, 1));
  }
}

function lastOf(days: number, yesterday: string): ResolvedRange {
  return { start: addDays(yesterday, -(days - 1)), end: yesterday };
}

function todayIn(timezone: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  // en-CA formats as YYYY-MM-DD; Intl is Node's only zoned-date path.
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
    timeZone: timezone,
    year: 'numeric',
    month: '2-digit',
    day: '2-digit',
  }).format(now);
}

function parts(date: string): [number, number, number] {
  const [y, m, d] = date.split('-').map(Number);
  return [y, m, d];
}

function ymd(y: number, m: number, d: number): string {
  return `${y}-${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function daysInMonth(y: number, m: number): number {
  return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate();
}

function isLastDayOfMonth(date: string): boolean {
  const [y, m, d] = parts(date);
  return d === daysInMonth(y, m);
}

function shiftYear(date: string, toMonthEnd: boolean): string {
  const [y, m, d] = parts(date);
  const last = daysInMonth(y - 1, m);
  return ymd(y - 1, m, toMonthEnd ? last : Math.min(d, last));
}

function addDays(date: string, days: number): string {
  const [y, m, d] = parts(date);
  const t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
  t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + days);
  return ymd(t.getUTCFullYear(), t.getUTCMonth() + 1, t.getUTCDate());
}

function daysBetween(start: string, end: string): number {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(`${end}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${start}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000);
}
```

Create `src/tool-input.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { RELATIVE_RANGES } from './dates.js';

// Input building blocks shared by tools, so every tool that takes a range
// accepts and documents the same shape (spec §3.2).

// A YYYY-MM-DD date. Any string parses here: the date rules belong to
// resolveDateRange, so a bad date is INVALID_DATE_RANGE naming its field,
// never a schema error.
export function dateField(description: string): z.ZodString {
  return z.string().describe(description);
}

// Either { start, end } or { relative }.
export function dateRangeInput(description: string) {
  return z
    .union([
      z
        .object({
          start: dateField('First day of the range, inclusive, as YYYY-MM-DD.'),
          end: dateField('Last day of the range, inclusive, as YYYY-MM-DD.'),
        })
        .strict()
        .describe('Absolute dates.'),
      z
        .object({
          relative: z
            .enum(RELATIVE_RANGES)
            .describe(
              'A range relative to today in the date time zone (GAM_TIMEZONE). Every choice ends by yesterday; ' +
              'month_to_date, quarter_to_date and year_to_date fail on the first day of their period.',
            ),
        })
        .strict()
        .describe('A relative range.'),
    ])
    .describe(description);
}
```

```ts
// Shared numeric helpers for diagnostic tools that need raw rows (rather
// than the envelope buildReportTool produces) so they can post-process —
// funnel attribution, floor-pricing classification, etc.
import type { Cell } from './values.js';

// ponytail: missing values still count as 0 here; 0.6.0 PR 4 replaces this with null-propagating arithmetic.
export function numOr0(v: Cell | undefined): number {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

export function pct(fraction: number): string {
  return (fraction * 100).toFixed(2);
}
```

5. Replace:

```ts

function resolveComparison(
  base: DateRange,
  cmp: 'previous_period' | 'previous_year' | { start: string; end: string },
): DateRange {
  if (typeof cmp === 'object') return { start: cmp.start, end: cmp.end };
  const start = new Date(`${base.start}T00:00:00Z`);
  const end = new Date(`${base.end}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (cmp === 'previous_year') {
    start.setUTCFullYear(start.getUTCFullYear() - 1);
    end.setUTCFullYear(end.getUTCFullYear() - 1);
    return { start: iso(start), end: iso(end) };
  }
  // previous_period: end = base.start - 1 day; start = end - (base length).
  const oneDay = 86_400_000;
  const lengthMs = end.getTime() - start.getTime();
  const prevEnd = new Date(start.getTime() - oneDay);
  const prevStart = new Date(prevEnd.getTime() - lengthMs);
  return { start: iso(prevStart), end: iso(prevEnd) };
}

function iso(d: Date): string {
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
```

with:

```ts

// Both are computed from a checked base range, so they end before it does.
function comparisonRange(base: ResolvedRange, comparison: 'previous_period' | 'previous_year'): ResolvedRange {
  return comparison === 'previous_year' ? previousYear(base) : previousPeriod(base);
}
```

```ts
};

function customRange(
  baseline: DateRange,
  lookbackDays: number,
  timezone: string,
  now: Date | undefined,
): DateRange {
  if (lookbackDays === 7 || lookbackDays === 30) return baseline;
  // Build [yesterday - (lookback-1) days .. yesterday] in the network tz.
  const today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
    timeZone: timezone, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
  }).format(now ?? new Date());
  const [y, m, d] = today.split('-').map(Number);
  const yest = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d - 1));
  const start = new Date(yest.getTime() - (lookbackDays - 1) * 86_400_000);
  const iso = (dt: Date) => dt.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  return { start: iso(start), end: iso(yest) };
}
```

---

### Task 4: The advertised schema says everything, and an input error names the problem (spec §3.1)

The hand-built zod→JSON-Schema converter moves out of `src/server.ts` into `src/json-schema.ts` and is extended (spec D-5): `description`, `default`, `minimum`/`maximum` (or the exclusive forms), `minLength`/`maxLength`, `minItems`/`maxItems`, `pattern`, `enum`, `const`, `integer`, `format: date` with its pattern, and `additionalProperties: false` for an object that is really strict (it reads zod's `unknownKeys`, so the advertised schema cannot claim a strictness the parser lacks). A zod type or check it cannot describe throws, so a new kind of field cannot silently become `{}`.

Date fields are marked by `dateField` in a module-level `WeakSet`, because they must stay plain strings for the parser (Task 3). A later `.describe()` on a date field would make an unmarked copy; `dateField` takes the description for that reason.

`describeInputError` turns a zod error into one line: `unknown key "filter"`, `date_range: unknown key "tz"` (for a union it reports the choice the input came closest to), `n: Expected number, received string`. The server uses it for `INVALID_INPUT`.

No tool input changes in this task; Task 7 makes them strict and described.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/json-schema.ts`, `tests/json-schema.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/server.ts`, `src/tool-input.ts`, `tests/server-errors.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dateRangeInput` (Task 3), `ALL_TOOLS`, `buildMcpServer`.
- Produces:
  - `src/json-schema.ts`: `type JsonSchema = { [key: string]: unknown }`; `toJsonSchema(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): JsonSchema`.
  - `src/tool-input.ts`: `isDateField(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): boolean`; `describeInputError(error: z.ZodError): string`; `dateField` now registers the field.
  - `src/server.ts`: `zodToJsonSchema` removed; tools/list uses `toJsonSchema`; `INVALID_INPUT` reasons use `describeInputError`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The expected `pattern` of a date field is the JSON text `^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$`, written in TypeScript as `'^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}-\\\\d{2}$'` (two backslashes before each `d`). If a backslash is lost when the file is written, this test fails; fix the file rather than the test.

Create `tests/json-schema.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { toJsonSchema } from '../src/json-schema.js';
import { dateField, dateRangeInput, describeInputError } from '../src/tool-input.js';
import { ALL_TOOLS } from '../src/tools/index.js';

describe('toJsonSchema (spec §3.1)', () => {
  it('advertises descriptions, defaults, bounds, patterns, enums, dates and strictness', () => {
    const schema = z.object({
      name: z.string().min(1).max(64).regex(/^[a-z]+$/).describe('A name.'),
      top_n: z.number().int().min(1).max(500).default(50).describe('How many rows.'),
      ratio: z.number().positive().lt(1).optional().describe('A share.'),
      kind: z.enum(['a', 'b']).default('a').describe('Which kind.'),
      ids: z.array(z.number().int().positive()).min(1).max(50).optional().describe('Some IDs.'),
      day: dateField('A day.'),
      flag: z.boolean().optional().describe('A switch.'),
      pick: z.union([z.literal('x'), z.object({ y: z.string().describe('Y.') }).strict()]).describe('One or the other.'),
    }).strict();

    expect(toJsonSchema(schema)).toEqual({
      type: 'object',
      properties: {
        name: { description: 'A name.', type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 64, pattern: '^[a-z]+$' },
        top_n: { description: 'How many rows.', type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: 500, default: 50 },
        ratio: { description: 'A share.', type: 'number', exclusiveMinimum: 0, exclusiveMaximum: 1 },
        kind: { description: 'Which kind.', type: 'string', enum: ['a', 'b'], default: 'a' },
        ids: {
          description: 'Some IDs.',
          type: 'array',
          items: { type: 'integer', exclusiveMinimum: 0 },
          minItems: 1,
          maxItems: 50,
        },
        day: { description: 'A day.', type: 'string', format: 'date', pattern: '^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$' },
        flag: { description: 'A switch.', type: 'boolean' },
        pick: {
          description: 'One or the other.',
          anyOf: [
            { const: 'x' },
            { type: 'object', properties: { y: { description: 'Y.', type: 'string' } }, required: ['y'], additionalProperties: false },
          ],
        },
      },
      required: ['name', 'day', 'pick'],
      additionalProperties: false,
    });
  });

  it('leaves additionalProperties out for an object that is not strict', () => {
    expect(toJsonSchema(z.object({}))).toEqual({ type: 'object', properties: {} });
  });

  it('keeps a description given after optional() or default()', () => {
    expect(toJsonSchema(z.string().optional().describe('Later.'))).toEqual({ description: 'Later.', type: 'string' });
    expect(toJsonSchema(z.number().default(3).describe('Later.'))).toEqual({ description: 'Later.', type: 'number', default: 3 });
  });

  it('refuses a zod type it cannot describe', () => {
    expect(() => toJsonSchema(z.object({ when: z.date() }))).toThrow('no JSON Schema for zod type ZodDate');
    expect(() => toJsonSchema(z.string().email())).toThrow('no JSON Schema for string check email');
  });

  it('describes every registered tool input', () => {
    for (const tool of ALL_TOOLS) {
      expect(() => toJsonSchema(tool.input), tool.name).not.toThrow();
      expect(toJsonSchema(tool.input)).toMatchObject({ type: 'object' });
    }
  });
});

describe('describeInputError', () => {
  const input = z.object({ date_range: dateRangeInput('Dates.'), top_n: z.number() }).strict();
  const reason = (value: unknown) => {
    const parsed = input.safeParse(value);
    if (parsed.success) throw new Error('expected a parse failure');
    return describeInputError(parsed.error);
  };
  const range = { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-02' };

  it('names an unknown top-level key', () => {
    expect(reason({ date_range: range, top_n: 1, filter: [] })).toBe('unknown key "filter"');
  });

  it('names an unknown key inside a range, from the choice the input came closest to', () => {
    expect(reason({ date_range: { ...range, tz: 'UTC' }, top_n: 1 })).toBe('date_range: unknown key "tz"');
  });

  it('names a relative range that does not exist', () => {
    expect(reason({ date_range: { relative: 'today' }, top_n: 1 })).toMatch(/^date_range\.relative: Invalid enum value\. .*received 'today'$/);
  });

  it('joins several problems', () => {
    expect(reason({ date_range: range, top_n: 'x', a: 1, b: 2 })).toBe('top_n: Expected number, received string; unknown keys "a", "b"');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the converter and the error text, and use them in the server**

`DATE_PATTERN` is written with two backslashes before each `d` (the TypeScript source of `^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$`). Check it after writing the file: the Step 1 test compares it.

Create `src/json-schema.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { isDateField } from './tool-input.js';

export type JsonSchema = { [key: string]: unknown };

const DATE_PATTERN = '^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$';

// The advertised input schema of a tool (spec §3.1): descriptions, defaults,
// bounds, patterns, enums, `format: date` and `additionalProperties: false`
// for strict objects. Hand-built because SDK v1 has no converter of its own
// (spec D-5). A zod type it does not know throws, so a new kind of field
// cannot be advertised as an empty schema that accepts anything.
export function toJsonSchema(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): JsonSchema {
  const { description: inner, ...node } = convert(schema);
  const description = schema.description ?? inner;
  return description === undefined ? node : { description, ...node };
}

function convert(s: z.ZodTypeAny): JsonSchema {
  if (s instanceof z.ZodOptional) return toJsonSchema(s.unwrap());
  if (s instanceof z.ZodDefault) return { ...toJsonSchema(s._def.innerType), default: s._def.defaultValue() };
  if (s instanceof z.ZodEffects) return toJsonSchema(s.innerType());
  if (s instanceof z.ZodObject) return objectSchema(s);
  if (s instanceof z.ZodString) return stringSchema(s);
  if (s instanceof z.ZodNumber) return numberSchema(s);
  if (s instanceof z.ZodBoolean) return { type: 'boolean' };
  if (s instanceof z.ZodEnum) return { type: 'string', enum: [...s.options] };
  if (s instanceof z.ZodLiteral) return { const: s.value };
  if (s instanceof z.ZodArray) return arraySchema(s);
  if (s instanceof z.ZodUnion) return { anyOf: s.options.map((o: z.ZodTypeAny) => toJsonSchema(o)) };
  throw new Error(`toJsonSchema: no JSON Schema for zod type ${String(s._def.typeName)}`);
}

function objectSchema(s: z.ZodObject<z.ZodRawShape>): JsonSchema {
  const properties: Record<string, JsonSchema> = {};
  const required: string[] = [];
  for (const [key, field] of Object.entries(s.shape)) {
    properties[key] = toJsonSchema(field);
    if (!field.isOptional()) required.push(key);
  }
  return {
    type: 'object',
    properties,
    ...(required.length > 0 && { required }),
    ...(s._def.unknownKeys === 'strict' && { additionalProperties: false }),
  };
}

function stringSchema(s: z.ZodString): JsonSchema {
  if (isDateField(s)) return { type: 'string', format: 'date', pattern: DATE_PATTERN };
  const out: JsonSchema = { type: 'string' };
  for (const check of s._def.checks) {
    if (check.kind === 'min') out.minLength = check.value;
    else if (check.kind === 'max') out.maxLength = check.value;
    else if (check.kind === 'regex') out.pattern = check.regex.source;
    else throw new Error(`toJsonSchema: no JSON Schema for string check ${check.kind}`);
  }
  return out;
}

function numberSchema(s: z.ZodNumber): JsonSchema {
  const out: JsonSchema = { type: s.isInt ? 'integer' : 'number' };
  for (const check of s._def.checks) {
    if (check.kind === 'min') out[check.inclusive ? 'minimum' : 'exclusiveMinimum'] = check.value;
    else if (check.kind === 'max') out[check.inclusive ? 'maximum' : 'exclusiveMaximum'] = check.value;
    else if (check.kind !== 'int') throw new Error(`toJsonSchema: no JSON Schema for number check ${check.kind}`);
  }
  return out;
}

function arraySchema(s: z.ZodArray<z.ZodTypeAny>): JsonSchema {
  const { minLength, maxLength, exactLength } = s._def;
  return {
    type: 'array',
    items: toJsonSchema(s.element),
    ...((exactLength ?? minLength) && { minItems: (exactLength ?? minLength)!.value }),
    ...((exactLength ?? maxLength) && { maxItems: (exactLength ?? maxLength)!.value }),
  };
}
```

```ts
// A YYYY-MM-DD date. Any string parses here: the date rules belong to
```

```ts
// accepts and documents the same shape (spec §3.2).

// Date fields, so the advertised schema can say `format: date` without
// zod rejecting a bad date before resolveDateRange can name it.
const DATE_FIELDS = new WeakSet<z.ZodTypeAny>();

// A YYYY-MM-DD date. Any string parses here: the date rules belong to
// resolveDateRange, so a bad date is INVALID_DATE_RANGE naming its field,
// never a schema error. Describe it here: a later .describe() makes a copy
// that is no longer marked as a date.
export function dateField(description: string): z.ZodString {
  const field = z.string().describe(description);
  DATE_FIELDS.add(field);
  return field;
}

export function isDateField(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): boolean {
  return DATE_FIELDS.has(schema);
}
```

```ts
// A failed parse as one line naming where each problem is, for
// INVALID_INPUT: `date_range: unknown key "tz"`.
export function describeInputError(error: z.ZodError): string {
  return error.issues.map(describeIssue).join('; ');
}

function describeIssue(issue: z.ZodIssue): string {
  if (issue.code === 'invalid_union') {
    // Name the problems of the choice the input came closest to.
    const closest = [...issue.unionErrors].sort((a, b) => a.issues.length - b.issues.length)[0];
    if (closest) return describeInputError(closest);
  }
  const at = issue.path.length > 0 ? `${issue.path.join('.')}: ` : '';
  if (issue.code === 'unrecognized_keys') {
    const keys = issue.keys.map((k) => JSON.stringify(k)).join(', ');
    return `${at}unknown ${issue.keys.length === 1 ? 'key' : 'keys'} ${keys}`;
  }
  return `${at}${issue.message}`;
}
```

```ts
// Minimal zod → JSON Schema. zod has many third-party converters; for v1 we
// emit a permissive schema that lets the LLM at least see field names.
function zodToJsonSchema(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): unknown {
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodObject) {
    const shape = (schema as z.ZodObject<any>).shape;
    const properties: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    const required: string[] = [];
    for (const key of Object.keys(shape)) {
      const sub = shape[key];
      properties[key] = zodToJsonSchema(sub);
      if (!sub.isOptional()) required.push(key);
    }
    return { type: 'object', properties, required };
  }
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodString) return { type: 'string' };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodNumber) return { type: 'number' };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodBoolean) return { type: 'boolean' };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodArray) return { type: 'array', items: zodToJsonSchema((schema as z.ZodArray<any>).element) };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodEnum) return { type: 'string', enum: (schema as z.ZodEnum<any>).options };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodOptional) return zodToJsonSchema((schema as z.ZodOptional<any>).unwrap());
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodUnion) return { anyOf: (schema as z.ZodUnion<any>).options.map(zodToJsonSchema) };
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodDefault) return zodToJsonSchema((schema as any)._def.innerType);
  // ZodEffects wraps the schema in .refine/.transform/.preprocess; descend.
  if (schema instanceof z.ZodEffects) return zodToJsonSchema((schema as z.ZodEffects<any>).innerType());
  return {};
}
```

### Task 5: Every report query is checked against its report type before GAM sees it (spec §2.1 step 1, §4.4, H3)

`checkReportQuery` (in `src/schema.ts`) refuses a query that names an unknown dimension, metric or filter dimension, or a name its report type does not offer. A schema entry listed with no report types (the publisher-configured slots such as `CUSTOM_DIMENSION_0_VALUE`) is not restricted, as in `resolveReportType`. `executeReport` and `executeReportRows` run it first and return `INVALID_INPUT` without touching GAM, so every tool — the builder tools included — is covered in one place (spec §2.1 step 1; §4.4's "`buildReportTool` runs `validateCombo`" falls out of it).

H3 needs every name a tool can request to exist in `schema-generated.ts` for its report type. `inventory_breakdown` asks for `TOTAL_LINE_ITEM_LEVEL_REVENUE` and `TOTAL_LINE_ITEM_LEVEL_IMPRESSIONS`, which do not exist, so this task switches it to `REVENUE` and `IMPRESSIONS` (spec §4.4, I8; Task 1 moved I8 to this PR).

`tests/tool-table.ts` is new: one row of valid arguments per registered tool, and `toolWorld()`, a context in which every row succeeds (the entities, line item and saved query the rows name exist, and any report definition is answered with two empty rows). H3 crosses each tool's row with every combination of its enum fields, runs the handler, and checks every definition it built. Tasks 7–9 and 14 reuse the table; a registered tool without a row fails them.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/tool-requests.test.ts`, `tests/tool-table.ts`
- Modify: `src/report-exec.ts`, `src/schema.ts`, `src/tools/inventory_breakdown.ts`, `tests/tools/inventory_breakdown.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `validateCombo`, `reportTypesFor`, `isValidDimension` (`src/schema.ts`), `GENERATED_DIMENSIONS`, `GENERATED_METRICS`, `makeCtx`, `counted`, `encodeCursor`, `createUsageCounters`.
- Produces:
  - `src/schema.ts`: `checkReportQuery(q: { dimensions: string[]; metrics: string[]; filters?: { field: string }[]; report_type?: string }): string | null`.
  - `src/report-exec.ts` (module-private): `queryProblem(query: ReportQuery): GamErrorEnvelope | null`, called first by `executeReport` and `executeReportRows`.
  - `tests/tool-table.ts`: `VALID_ARGS: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>`; `argsFor(tool: string): Record<string, unknown>` (a copy; throws for a tool without a row); `NO_REPORT: Set<string>`; `toolWorld(over?: Partial<ToolContext>): ToolContext`.

```ts
// One row per registered tool: arguments that parse, and that succeed
// against toolWorld(). The guard tests (H1, H2, H3, H5, key order) iterate
// the tools the server registers and look each one up here, so a tool added
// without a row fails them rather than being skipped.
import type { ReportFixture } from '../src/gam-client.js';
import { definitionRange } from '../src/report-definition.js';
import type { ToolContext } from '../src/report-tool-builder.js';
import type { SavedQueryEntry, SavedQueryStore } from '../src/saved-queries.js';
import { createUsageCounters } from '../src/usage.js';
import { encodeCursor } from '../src/cursor.js';
import { makeCtx, NETWORK } from './helpers.js';

// makeCtx's clock: 2026-09-17 in Asia/Tokyo.
const PAST = { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-07' };
const FUTURE = { start: '2026-09-21', end: '2026-09-27' };

export const VALID_ARGS: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> = {
  lookup: { entity_type: 'ad_unit', query: 'home', limit: 10, match: 'substring' },
  describe_schema: { kind: 'metric', search: 'impressions', limit: 5 },
  describe_line_item: { ids: [1] },
  run_report: {
    dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'],
    metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'],
    date_range: PAST,
    filters: [{ field: 'AD_UNIT_ID', op: 'IN', value: [1] }],
  },
  fetch_report_page: {
    cursor: encodeCursor({
      result: `networks/${NETWORK}/reports/1/results/1`,
      page_token: '1',
      offset: 1,
      total_row_count: 2,
      run_time: null,
      date_range: null,
    }),
  },
  revenue_summary: { date_range: PAST, group_by: 'day' },
  top_advertisers: { date_range: PAST, top_n: 5, metric: 'revenue' },
  top_line_items: { date_range: PAST, top_n: 5, metric: 'impressions', line_item_type: 'STANDARD' },
  pacing_status: { line_item_ids: [1], order_ids: [2] },
  fill_rate: { date_range: PAST, group_by: 'ad_unit' },
  inventory_breakdown: { date_range: PAST, group_by: 'order' },
  revenue_trend: { date_range: PAST, granularity: 'week', split_by: 'country' },
  compare_periods: {
    dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'],
    metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'],
    base_range: PAST,
    comparison: 'previous_period',
    top_n: 5,
  },
  find_anomalies: { kind: 'fill_rate_drops', threshold_pct: 10, top_n: 5 },
  kv_breakdown: { custom_targeting_key_id: 11, date_range: PAST, metric: 'revenue', top_n: 5, ad_unit_id: 1 },
  kv_report: { kv: 'section=news', date_range: PAST, metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'], dimensions: ['DATE'] },
  bidder_breakdown: { date_range: PAST, group_by: 'day', top_n: 5 },
  yield_breakdown: { date_range: PAST, by: 'buyer', group_by: 'country', top_n: 5 },
  video_breakdown: { date_range: PAST, by: 'placement', group_by: 'device', top_n: 5 },
  viewability_breakdown: { date_range: PAST, by: 'size', group_by: 'day', top_n: 5 },
  reach_report: { date_range: PAST, by: 'order', group_by: 'country', ids: [2] },
  ad_speed: { date_range: PAST, by: 'device', focus: 'page_timing' },
  sell_through: { date_range: FUTURE, by: 'placement', ids: [3] },
  consent_messaging: { date_range: PAST, focus: 'att', group_by: 'os_group' },
  find_unused_ad_units: { lookback_days: 14, min_requests: 10, max_units: 100 },
  diagnose_fill_rate: { ad_unit_query: 'home', lookback_days: 7, comparison: 'none', top_n: 5, min_requests: 100 },
  diagnose_floor_pricing: { ad_unit_ids: [1], lookback_days: 7, min_requests: 100, top_n: 5 },
  health_check: {},
  usage_stats: {},
  save_query: {
    name: 'weekly_units',
    description: 'Impressions by ad unit.',

  },

  run_saved_query: { name: 'weekly_units', date_range: { relative: 'last_7_days' } },
  delete_saved_query: { name: 'weekly_units' },
};

// A copy of a tool's row; throws for a tool without one.
export function argsFor(tool: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  const args = VALID_ARGS[tool];
  if (!args) throw new Error(`tests/tool-table.ts has no valid arguments for ${tool}: add a row`);
  return structuredClone(args);
}

// Tools that never build a report definition.
export const NO_REPORT = new Set([
  'lookup', 'describe_schema', 'describe_line_item', 'fetch_report_page', 'health_check', 'usage_stats',
  'save_query', 'list_saved_queries', 'delete_saved_query',
]);

function memorySavedQueries(): SavedQueryStore {
  const entries = new Map<string, SavedQueryEntry>([[
    'weekly_units',
    { name: 'weekly_units', saved_at: '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z', body: { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] } },
  ]]);
  return {
    path: '(memory)',
    save: (name, body, description) => {
      const entry = { name, body, saved_at: '2026-09-17T04:00:00Z', ...(description && { description }) };
      entries.set(name, entry);
      return entry;
    },
    get: (name) => entries.get(name) ?? null,
    list: () => [...entries.values()],
    delete: (name) => entries.delete(name),
  };
}

// A context in which every row of VALID_ARGS succeeds: the entities and
// resources the rows name exist, a saved query is stored, and any report
// definition is answered with two rows whose cells are all empty.
export function toolWorld(over: Partial<ToolContext> = {}): ToolContext {
  const reports: ReportFixture[] = [];
  const ctx = makeCtx(
    {
      reports,
      entities: {
        ad_unit: [{ id: 1, name: 'Home top' }],
        company: [{ id: 5, name: 'Example advertiser' }],
        custom_targeting_key: [{ id: 11, name: 'section', displayName: 'Section' }],
      },
      resources: { 'lineItems/1': { name: `networks/${NETWORK}/lineItems/1`, displayName: 'Example line item' } },
    },
    { usageCounters: createUsageCounters(), savedQueries: memorySavedQueries(), ...over },
  );
  const inner = ctx.client;
  ctx.client = {
    ...inner,
    defineReport: (definition) => {
      const dateRange = definitionRange(definition) ?? undefined;
      reports.push({ query: { dimensions: definition.dimensions, metrics: definition.metrics }, dateRange, rows: [{}, {}] });
      return inner.defineReport(definition);
    },
  };
  return ctx;
}
```

Create `tests/tool-requests.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ALL_TOOLS } from '../src/tools/index.js';
import { GENERATED_DIMENSIONS, GENERATED_METRICS, type GeneratedEntry } from '../src/schema-generated.js';
import { executeReport, executeReportRows } from '../src/report-exec.js';
import type { ReportDefinition } from '../src/report-definition.js';
import type { ToolDefinition } from '../src/report-tool-builder.js';
import { argsFor, NO_REPORT, toolWorld } from './tool-table.js';
import { counted } from './helpers.js';

const DIMENSIONS = new Map(GENERATED_DIMENSIONS.map((e) => [e.name, e]));
const METRICS = new Map(GENERATED_METRICS.map((e) => [e.name, e]));

// Every choice of an enum field (and leaving an optional one out).
function choicesOf(field: z.ZodTypeAny): unknown[] | null {
  let inner = field;
  let optional = false;
  for (;;) {
    if (inner instanceof z.ZodOptional) { optional = true; inner = inner.unwrap(); }
    else if (inner instanceof z.ZodDefault) inner = inner._def.innerType;
    else break;
  }
  if (!(inner instanceof z.ZodEnum)) return null;
  return optional ? [...inner.options, undefined] : [...inner.options];
}

// The tool's row from the table, crossed with every combination of its
// enum fields: together these reach every name the tool can request.
function variants(tool: ToolDefinition<z.ZodTypeAny>): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  let out = [argsFor(tool.name)];
  for (const [key, field] of Object.entries((tool.input as z.AnyZodObject).shape)) {
    const choices = choicesOf(field as z.ZodTypeAny);
    if (!choices) continue;
    out = out.flatMap((args) => choices.map((choice) => {
      const next = { ...args };
      if (choice === undefined) delete next[key];
      else next[key] = choice;
      return next;
    }));
  }
  return out;
}

function offered(entry: GeneratedEntry | undefined, reportType: string): boolean {
  // An entry listed without report types (a publisher-configured slot) is
  // not restricted.
  return entry !== undefined && (entry.report_types.length === 0 || entry.report_types.includes(reportType));
}

function filterFields(definition: ReportDefinition): string[] {
  return (definition.filters ?? []).map((f) => (f as { fieldFilter: { field: { dimension: string } } }).fieldFilter.field.dimension);
}

describe('H3: every name a tool can request exists for its report type', () => {
  it.each(ALL_TOOLS.map((t) => [t.name, t] as const))('%s', async (name, tool) => {
    const seen: ReportDefinition[] = [];
    for (const args of variants(tool)) {
      const ctx = toolWorld();
      const inner = ctx.client;
      ctx.client = { ...inner, defineReport: (d) => { seen.push(d); return inner.defineReport(d); } };
      const r = await tool.handler(tool.input.parse(args), ctx);
      // A report tool must get as far as GAM with every variant.
      if (!NO_REPORT.has(name)) expect(r, `${name} ${JSON.stringify(args)}`).not.toHaveProperty('error');
    }
    if (NO_REPORT.has(name)) {
      expect(seen).toEqual([]);
      return;
    }
    expect(seen.length, `${name} built no report`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const d of seen) {
      for (const dim of [...d.dimensions, ...filterFields(d)]) {
        expect(offered(DIMENSIONS.get(dim), d.reportType), `${name}: dimension ${dim} in ${d.reportType}`).toBe(true);
      }
      for (const metric of d.metrics) {
        expect(offered(METRICS.get(metric), d.reportType), `${name}: metric ${metric} in ${d.reportType}`).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe('the report path checks every query before GAM sees it (spec §2.1 step 1)', () => {
  const range = { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-07' };
  const cases = [
    [{ dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['TOTAL_LINE_ITEM_LEVEL_REVENUE'] }, 'Unknown metric: TOTAL_LINE_ITEM_LEVEL_REVENUE'],
    [{ dimensions: ['NOT_A_DIMENSION'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'] }, 'Unknown dimension: NOT_A_DIMENSION'],
    [{ dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['UNIQUE_VISITORS'] }, 'UNIQUE_VISITORS is not offered in HISTORICAL reports'],
    [
      { dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID'], metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS'], filters: [{ field: 'NOT_A_DIMENSION', op: '=' as const, value: 1 }] },
      'Unknown filter dimension: NOT_A_DIMENSION',
    ],
  ] as const;

  it.each(cases)('%j', async (query, reason) => {
    const { ctx, counters } = counted({});
    for (const r of [
      await executeReport(ctx, { tool: 't', query: structuredClone(query) as never, dateRange: range, summary: 'S' }),
      await executeReportRows(ctx, structuredClone(query) as never, range, 't'),
    ]) {
      expect(r).toMatchObject({ error: { code: 'INVALID_INPUT', details: { reason } } });
    }
    expect(counters.report_definitions_created).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
// What is wrong with a query for its report type, or null. Every report the
// report path builds passes this first (spec §2.1 step 1, test H3): each
// dimension, metric and filter field exists, and is offered under the
// report type. An entry the schema lists without report types (the
// publisher-configured slots) is not restricted, as in resolveReportType.
export function checkReportQuery(q: {
  dimensions: string[];
  metrics: string[];
  filters?: { field: string }[];
  report_type?: string;
}): string | null {
  const unknown = validateCombo(q);
  if (unknown) return unknown;
  const filterFields = (q.filters ?? []).map((f) => f.field);
  const badFilter = filterFields.find((f) => !isValidDimension(f));
  if (badFilter !== undefined) return `Unknown filter dimension: ${badFilter}`;
  const type = q.report_type ?? 'HISTORICAL';
  for (const name of [...q.dimensions, ...q.metrics, ...filterFields]) {
    const types = reportTypesFor(name);
    if (types.length > 0 && !types.includes(type)) return `${name} is not offered in ${type} reports`;
  }
  return null;
}
```

```ts
// A query its report type cannot run is refused before anything reaches GAM.
function queryProblem(query: ReportQuery): GamErrorEnvelope | null {

  return problem === null
    ? null
    : gamError('INVALID_INPUT', {
```

---

### Task 6: Report tools take any date range and check it by report type (F4, I5, I14)

`buildReportTool` now resolves `input.date_range` with `resolveDateRange` under `rangeRuleFor(query.report_type)` before anything runs, and hands the resolved range to `summary(input, range)`. Its input type is `ReportToolInput`, so a tool built with it must have a `date_range` (a builder tool without one does not typecheck). The unused `buildDateRange` option is removed.

All fifteen builder tools switch `date_range` to `dateRangeInput(...)`, so they accept relative ranges too, and reach their final input form here: `.strict()` and a description on every field (Task 7 does the same for the other tools). `HISTORICAL_RANGE` is the shared description of a historical `date_range`. Only `sell_through` runs a `FUTURE_SELL_THROUGH` report, so only it gets the forecast rule; its description says relative ranges are refused.

The per-tool rule table before Task 1 lists every file. `top_advertisers`, `inventory_breakdown`, `fill_rate`, `top_line_items`, `revenue_summary` and `revenue_trend` are short and shown whole; the others are shown as edits.

`tests/date-inputs.test.ts` stops listing tools: it finds every field advertised as a date range and checks each (19 fields), so a new range-taking tool is covered without being listed. The two `sell_through` tests that used past windows move to future ones.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/report-tool-builder.ts`, `src/tool-input.ts`, `src/tools/ad_speed.ts`, `src/tools/bidder_breakdown.ts`, `src/tools/consent_messaging.ts`, `src/tools/fill_rate.ts`, `src/tools/inventory_breakdown.ts`, `src/tools/kv_breakdown.ts`, `src/tools/reach_report.ts`, `src/tools/revenue_summary.ts`, `src/tools/revenue_trend.ts`, `src/tools/sell_through.ts`, `src/tools/top_advertisers.ts`, `src/tools/top_line_items.ts`, `src/tools/video_breakdown.ts`, `src/tools/viewability_breakdown.ts`, `src/tools/yield_breakdown.ts`, `tests/date-inputs.test.ts`, `tests/report-tool-builder.test.ts`, `tests/tools/sell_through.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `resolveDateRange`, `rangeRuleFor`, `DateRangeInput`, `ResolvedRange` (Task 3), `dateRangeInput`, `toJsonSchema`, `argsFor`, `toolWorld`.
- Produces:
  - `src/report-tool-builder.ts`: `type ReportToolInput = z.ZodType<{ date_range: DateRangeInput }, z.ZodTypeDef, unknown>` (Task 7 narrows it to a strict object); `ReportToolConfig.summary: (input, range: ResolvedRange) => string`; `buildDateRange` removed; `buildReportTool` returns `INVALID_DATE_RANGE` before running anything.
  - `src/tool-input.ts`: `HISTORICAL_RANGE: string`.
