```ts
export type HttpSettings = {
  port: number;
  // GAM_HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN. Never logged or quoted.
  bearerToken: string | undefined;
  // GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS: lower-case host names; undefined means no Host check.
  allowedHosts: string[] | undefined;
};

const HTTP_ONLY = ['GAM_HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN', 'GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS'] as const;

// The HTTP settings (spec §2.7), or null when GAM_HTTP_PORT is unset or empty
// (stdio mode). Each error names its variable; none quotes the token.
export function readHttpSettings(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): HttpSettings | null | { error: string } {
  const rawPort = env.GAM_HTTP_PORT;
  if (rawPort === undefined || rawPort === '') return null;
  const port = wholeNumber(rawPort);
  if (port === null || port < 1 || port > 65_535) {
    return { error: `GAM_HTTP_PORT must be a valid port number, got ${JSON.stringify(rawPort)}` };
  }
  const bearerToken = env.GAM_HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN || undefined;
  if (bearerToken !== undefined && ![...bearerToken].every((c) => c > ' ' && c <= '~')) {
    return { error: 'GAM_HTTP_BEARER_TOKEN must be printable ASCII with no spaces' };
  }
  const allowedHosts = hostList(env.GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS);
  if (allowedHosts !== undefined && 'error' in allowedHosts) return allowedHosts;
  return { port, bearerToken, allowedHosts };
}

function hostList(raw: string | undefined): string[] | undefined | { error: string } {
  if (raw === undefined || raw.trim() === '') return undefined;
  const hosts = raw.split(',').map((h) => h.trim()).filter((h) => h !== '');
  if (hosts.length === 0) return { error: `GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS names no host: ${JSON.stringify(raw)}` };
  for (const host of hosts) {
    if (hostnameOf(host) !== host.toLowerCase()) {
      return {
        error: `GAM_HTTP_ALLOWED_HOSTS must list host names without ports, separated by commas; ${JSON.stringify(host)} is not one`,
      };
    }
  }
  return hosts.map((h) => h.toLowerCase());
}

// Settings that only HTTP mode reads, set in stdio mode.
export function ignoredInStdio(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): string[] {
  return HTTP_ONLY.filter((name) => env[name]);
}
```

### Task 14: Both transports work end to end (owner requirement)

Test only. For each transport the test drives the real server code through the real SDK transport, with the real REST client talking to `tests/fake-gam.ts` (no network, no credential), and does what a client does first: `initialize`, `tools/list` (33 tools with the saved-query tools over stdio; 29 without them over HTTP), `run_report` (typed rows, schema and provenance, a `next_cursor`), and `fetch_report_page` (the last row, `next_cursor: null`).

- **stdio:** `connectStdio(ctx, { stdin, stdout })` over two `PassThrough` streams, with an SDK `Client` on a small client transport that writes line-framed JSON-RPC to the server's stdin and reads its stdout with the SDK's own `ReadBuffer` and `serializeMessage`. The test also checks that stdout carried nothing but whole JSON-RPC lines. Spawning `dist/cli.js` instead is not hermetic: its startup check calls `networks.get` on the fixed API base URL, and making that point at the fake would need a test-only hook in production code, which this plan does not add.
- **HTTP:** the real `connectHttp` on an ephemeral port with a bearer token and a host list, and the SDK `StreamableHTTPClientTransport` sending the token through `requestInit`. A client without the token fails to connect.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/transports-e2e.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `connectStdio`, `buildMcpServer` (Task 8), `connectHttp` (Task 12), `createFakeGam`, `restCtx`, `createReportCache`, `payloadOf`.
- Produces: nothing new.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `tests/transports-e2e.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { PassThrough, type Readable, type Writable } from 'node:stream';
import { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';
import { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';
import { ReadBuffer, serializeMessage } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/shared/stdio.js';
import type { Transport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/shared/transport.js';
import type { JSONRPCMessage } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';
import { buildMcpServer, connectStdio } from '../src/server.js';
import { connectHttp } from '../src/http-transport.js';
import { createReportCache } from '../src/cache.js';
import { createLogger } from '../src/log.js';
import type { ToolContext } from '../src/report-tool-builder.js';
import { createFakeGam, dbl, int, str } from './fake-gam.js';
import { NETWORK, payloadOf, restCtx } from './helpers.js';

// The owner's requirement: 0.6.0 works over both MCP transports. Each case
// drives the real server code through the real SDK transport, with the real
// REST client talking to the in-process fake GAM, and checks the four things
// a client does first: initialize, list tools, run a report, page through it.

const TOKEN = 'e2e-token-not-a-secret-5f2a';
const SAVED_QUERY_TOOLS = ['save_query', 'list_saved_queries', 'run_saved_query', 'delete_saved_query'];
const RUN = {
  dimensions: ['AD_UNIT_ID', 'AD_UNIT_NAME'],
  metrics: ['IMPRESSIONS', 'REVENUE'],
  date_range: { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-07' },
};

// Three rows served two to a page, so the report has a second page.
function world(): ToolContext {
  const gam = createFakeGam();
  gam.setRows({
    byColumn: [
      { AD_UNIT_ID: int(101), AD_UNIT_NAME: str('Home top'), IMPRESSIONS: int(1500), REVENUE: dbl(12.5) },
      { AD_UNIT_ID: int(102), AD_UNIT_NAME: str('Article side'), IMPRESSIONS: int(900), REVENUE: dbl(3.25) },
      { AD_UNIT_ID: int(103), AD_UNIT_NAME: str('Footer'), IMPRESSIONS: int(40), REVENUE: dbl(0.1) },
    ],
  });
  const logger = createLogger({ level: 'error', sink: () => {} });
  return restCtx(gam, { pageRows: 2, logger, reportCache: createReportCache({ networkCode: NETWORK, logger }) });
}

// The client end of a stdio connection: line-framed JSON-RPC written to the
// server's stdin and read from its stdout, as a client that started the
// server as a child process sees it.
class StreamClientTransport implements Transport {
  onclose?: () => void;
  onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
  onmessage?: (message: JSONRPCMessage) => void;
  private readonly buffer = new ReadBuffer();

  constructor(private readonly toServer: Writable, private readonly fromServer: Readable) {}

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    this.fromServer.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
      try {
        this.buffer.append(chunk);
        for (let m = this.buffer.readMessage(); m !== null; m = this.buffer.readMessage()) this.onmessage?.(m);
      } catch (e) {
        this.onerror?.(e as Error);
      }
    });
  }

  async send(message: JSONRPCMessage): Promise<void> {
    this.toServer.write(serializeMessage(message));
  }

  async close(): Promise<void> {
    this.toServer.end();
    this.onclose?.();
  }
}

async function exercise(client: Client, mode: 'stdio' | 'http'): Promise<void> {
  expect(client.getServerVersion()?.name).toBe('gam-mcp');
  expect(client.getServerCapabilities()?.tools).toBeDefined();

  const names = (await client.listTools()).tools.map((t) => t.name);
  expect(names).toContain('run_report');
  expect(names).toContain('fetch_report_page');
  for (const name of SAVED_QUERY_TOOLS) {
    if (mode === 'stdio') expect(names).toContain(name);
    else expect(names).not.toContain(name);
  }
  expect(names).toHaveLength(mode === 'stdio' ? 33 : 29);

  const first = await client.callTool({ name: 'run_report', arguments: RUN });
  expect(first.isError, JSON.stringify(first.content)).toBeFalsy();
  const page1 = payloadOf(first);
  expect(page1.provenance).toMatchObject({
    network_code: NETWORK,
    report_type: 'HISTORICAL',
    report: `networks/${NETWORK}/reports/1`,
    result: `networks/${NETWORK}/reports/1/results/1`,
    date_range: { start: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-09-07' },
    report_time_zone: 'Asia/Tokyo',
    date_time_zone: 'Asia/Tokyo',
    currency_code: 'USD',
    dimensions: RUN.dimensions,
    metrics: RUN.metrics,
    cache: { hit: false },
  });
  expect(page1.schema).toEqual([
    { name: 'AD_UNIT_ID', type: 'integer' },
    { name: 'AD_UNIT_NAME', type: 'string' },
    { name: 'IMPRESSIONS', type: 'integer' },
    { name: 'REVENUE', type: 'number' },
  ]);
  expect(page1.rows).toEqual([
    { AD_UNIT_ID: 101, AD_UNIT_NAME: 'Home top', IMPRESSIONS: 1500, REVENUE: 12.5 },
    { AD_UNIT_ID: 102, AD_UNIT_NAME: 'Article side', IMPRESSIONS: 900, REVENUE: 3.25 },
  ]);
  expect(page1).toMatchObject({ total_row_count: 3, returned_row_count: 2, truncated: true });
  expect(page1.next_cursor).toEqual(expect.any(String));

  const second = await client.callTool({ name: 'fetch_report_page', arguments: { cursor: page1.next_cursor } });
  expect(second.isError, JSON.stringify(second.content)).toBeFalsy();
  const page2 = payloadOf(second);
  expect(page2.provenance).toMatchObject({ result: page1.provenance.result, report_time_zone: 'Asia/Tokyo' });
  expect(page2.rows).toEqual([{ AD_UNIT_ID: 103, AD_UNIT_NAME: 'Footer', IMPRESSIONS: 40, REVENUE: 0.1 }]);
  expect(page2).toMatchObject({ total_row_count: 3, returned_row_count: 1, truncated: false, next_cursor: null });
}

describe('both MCP transports, end to end (owner requirement)', () => {
  it('stdio: the stdio transport over in-process streams', async () => {
    const stdin = new PassThrough();
    const stdout = new PassThrough();
    const written: string[] = [];
    stdout.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => written.push(chunk.toString('utf8')));
    const server = await connectStdio(world(), { stdin, stdout });
    const client = new Client({ name: 'e2e-stdio', version: '1' });
    await client.connect(new StreamClientTransport(stdin, stdout));
    try {
      await exercise(client, 'stdio');
    } finally {
      await client.close();
      await server.close();
    }
    // stdout carries nothing but whole JSON-RPC messages, one per line.
    const text = written.join('');
    expect(text.endsWith('\n')).toBe(true);
    for (const line of text.slice(0, -1).split('\n')) expect(JSON.parse(line)).toMatchObject({ jsonrpc: '2.0' });
  });

  it('HTTP: connectHttp on an ephemeral port, with the bearer token', async () => {
    const ctx = world();
    const handle = await connectHttp(() => buildMcpServer(ctx, { mode: 'http' }), {
      port: 0,
      logger: ctx.logger,
      bearerToken: TOKEN,
      allowedHosts: ['127.0.0.1'],
    });
    const url = new URL(`http://127.0.0.1:${handle.port}/mcp`);
    const client = new Client({ name: 'e2e-http', version: '1' });
    try {
      const refused = new Client({ name: 'e2e-http-no-token', version: '1' });
      await expect(refused.connect(new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(url))).rejects.toThrow();

      await client.connect(new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(url, {
        requestInit: { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${TOKEN}` } },
      }));
      await exercise(client, 'http');
    } finally {
      await client.close();
      await handle.close();
    }
  });
});
```

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
import subprocess, sys
names = subprocess.run(
    ['git', 'diff', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=AM', 'feat/0.6.0-identity-cache...HEAD'],
    capture_output=True, text=True, check=True,
).stdout.split()
bad = []
for name in names:
    data = open(name, 'rb').read()
    for offset, byte in enumerate(data):
        if (byte < 32 and byte not in (9, 10, 13)) or byte == 127:
            bad.append(f'{name}: byte {byte} at offset {offset}')
            break
print('\n'.join(bad) if bad else f'no control bytes in {len(names)} changed files')
sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
EOF
```

Expected: `no control bytes in 81 changed files` (the count when this plan was written) and exit code 0.

### Choices beyond the letter of the spec (for the reviewer)

- **The Host check is this server's, not the transport's.** SDK 1.30 deprecates `allowedHosts` / `enableDnsRebindingProtection` and compares the whole header including the port; its replacement middleware is Express-only. Task 12 copies the middleware's semantics (`hostnameOf`) and Task 12 reworders spec §2.7. `/healthz` is exempt from the Host check as well as from the token, so a container probe addressing the pod by IP still works.
- **`openWorldHint` is declared even where the spec lists only `readOnlyHint`,** because MCP reads an absent `openWorldHint` as `true`, which would misdescribe `describe_schema`, `usage_stats`, `list_saved_queries`, `save_query` and `delete_saved_query`. `health_check` reaches GAM, so it is `READS_GAM`.
- **Date fields are plain strings in zod.** A `.regex()` would make `banana` an `INVALID_INPUT` schema error, but §3.2 says `INVALID_DATE_RANGE`; the advertised schema still says `format: date` and carries the pattern, through a marker `dateField` sets.
- **`checkReportQuery` lives in the report path,** not in each tool, so §2.1 step 1 holds for every caller including `buildReportTool`; the tools keep their own `validateCombo` calls, which fail earlier and with a tool-specific hint.
- **An entry the schema lists with no report types is unrestricted** (the publisher-configured `CUSTOM_DIMENSION_*` and `LINE_ITEM_CUSTOM_FIELD_*` slots), matching `resolveReportType`. H3 says so where it checks.
- **`report_time_zone` can be `null`** for a report created outside this server whose zone source is `AD_EXCHANGE`, unspecified or absent: a name this server cannot supply. The result carries a warning instead of a guess.
- **The bearer token is compared as SHA-256 digests.** `timingSafeEqual` throws on different lengths, and padding would leak the length; hashing gives both sides 32 bytes.
- **The pending entry is written back only when the key has no live entry.** A `done` entry another caller recorded, or a newer run, wins; an expired entry counts as absent.
- **`tests/tool-table.ts` is one table for arguments, annotations and a world where every tool succeeds.** Four guard tests (H1, H2, H3, the key-order test) and the end-to-end test read it, so a tool added without a row fails four tests rather than being skipped by all of them.

### Requirements not placed in a task

- **A real-process test for "a port already in use exits 2".** Reaching `listen` needs a credential that passes the startup check, and the API base URL is fixed, so a hermetic real-process test would need a test-only hook in production code. Task 12 tests the error itself (`HttpListenError` naming `GAM_HTTP_PORT`); Task 13's mapping to exit 2 is four lines in `main()`, next to the exits the real-process test does cover.
- **README, smoke script and release notes** for everything in this PR (the bearer token, the host list, the body limit, the stdio-only saved queries, the date rules): PR 5 per the plan index, which owns all documentation.
- **Spec §12-style acceptance of §4** — `top_n`, `derived`, pacing, heuristics, `health_check`'s `isError`, the bundled prompts: PR 4.
